H2KG Ontology

Class Index

Domain-specific ontology for hydrogen electrochemical systems, FAIR workflow representation, and external semantic alignment

Ontology release

Developed through DECODE, the Helmholtz Metadata Collaboration (HMC), and AIMWORKS to support FAIR ontology publication and reusable metadata infrastructure for hydrogen electrochemical systems.

DECODEHMCAIMWORKS
Home User Guide Overview Explore DECODE Workflows Reference Architecture Classes Properties Alignments Examples Import Import Catalog Quality TIB Benchmark Developer Release

Absorption Peak Wavelength

https://w3id.org/h2kg/hydrogen-ontology#AbsorptionPeakWavelength

A property denoting wavelength of UV–Vis absorption maxima (e.g., porphyrin Soret/Q bands, peaks observed in solid-state/solution).

Mappings: None
